After this: The Trisagion. “Our Father” with the exclamation. “Lord, have mercy” (12 times), and the prayer is said by the Priest: “O God and Lord of hosts,” and so forth.

The Ninth Hour, if it is celebrated together with the 6th Hour, beginneth with: “Come, let us worship” (thrice); if, however, this Hour is celebrated separately, then after “Blessed is our God” there is said also “O Heavenly King,” etc., as usual. Three psalms. “Glory,” and “Now and.” “Alleluia” (thrice). “Lord, have mercy” (thrice). Kathisma of the day. Troparion: “Thou Who at the ninth hour for our sake didst taste of death in the flesh,” etc. “Glory,” and “Now and”: “He Who for our sake,” etc. “Deliver us not up” is read. After this: The Trisagion. “Our Father” with the exclamation. Instead of the kontakion the troparion of the Fast: “When the thief beheld,” with “Glory,” and “Now and.” “Lord, have mercy” (40 times). And the Priest, having come forth, on the ambo saith the prayer: “Thou Who at all times.” “Lord, have mercy” (thrice). “Glory,” and “Now and”: “More honorable than the Cherubim,” etc., “In the name of the Lord bless, Father,” and so forth, whereunto the Priest before the royal doors saith: “God be bountiful unto us,” etc., if this Hour is celebrated separately; if, however, not:

then: “Through the prayers of our holy Fathers,” etc., and he maketh three prostrations,[^151] and there beginneth:

The Typika [*Obidnytsia*]. Here it is needful to note that it is celebrated either together with Vespers, or also with the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts. (If there be any feast, then first we say Ps. 102 [Ps. 102]. “Glory”: “Praise the Lord, O my soul.” “Now and”: “Only-begotten Son.”) If it is celebrated together with Vespers, then the Priest standeth before the royal doors, and the choir singeth: “In Thy kingdom.” (If there be any feast, then “In Thy kingdom” is only read.) After this they sing: “Remember us, O Lord,” and all make a bow. “Remember us, O Master,” and likewise they make a bow. Finally: “Remember us, O Holy One,” and they make a bow. After this there is read: “The choir of the holy Apostles.” The Creed (*Viruyu*). “Pardon, remit.” “Our Father” with the exclamation. The kontakion of the temple, of the day, and to the Saint. “Glory”: “With the Saints,” “Now and”: “O Protection of Christians.” “Lord, have mercy” (40 times). “Glory,” and “Now and.” “More honorable than the Cherubim,” etc. “In the name of the Lord,” etc., and the Priest, standing before the royal doors, proclaimeth: “Through the prayers of the holy Fathers,” and maketh 3 prostrations. The Trisagion. “Our Father” with the exclamation. “Lord, have mercy” (12 times), and then the prayer of the 9th Hour: “O Lord Jesus Christ,”[^152] etc. The smallest dismissal. The remaining parts of the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] are taken at the end of Vespers. (See p. 74.)

If, however, the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] is joined not only with Vespers, but also with the Liturgy of the Presanctified Gifts, then it is celebrated as was said above, only that it is needful to note that in this case, of the remaining parts, the prayer: “All-Holy Trinity” is omitted, and the other parts are taken at the end of the Liturgy.

§. 65.

The Hours of Bright Week.

They are celebrated in such wise as they are arranged in the *Molitvoslov* on pages 478–480, that is: after “Blessed is our God” there is taken: “Christ is risen” (thrice). “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ” (thrice). “They who preceded the dawn.” “Though Thou didst descend into the tomb.” “In the tomb bodily.” “Glory,” and “Now and.” “Lord, have mercy” (40 times). “Glory,” and “Now and.” “More honorable than the Cherubim.” “In the name of the Lord.” The Priest: “Through the prayers of our holy Fathers.” “Christ is risen” (thrice) and the dismissal. It is needful to note this, that all 4 Hours are concluded uniformly. — The Typika [*Obidnytsia*] is also celebrated as it is in the *Molitvoslov*, p. 480. That is, if there will be no Liturgy: “Bless the Lord, O my soul.” “Christ is risen” (thrice). “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ” (once). “They who preceded the dawn” (once). “Glory”: “Though Thou didst descend into the tomb.” “Now and”: “Only-begotten Son.” After this: The Beatitudes (*Blazhenny*), and that: from the Canon, Odes 3 and 6. The Prokimenon. The Epistle (*Apostol*), the Gospel. “Remember us, O Lord.” The Creed (*Viruyu*). “Pardon, remit.” “Our Father.” Kontakion: “Though Thou didst descend into the tomb.” “Lord, have mercy” (40 times). “All-Holy Trinity.” “Christ is risen” (thrice). Psalm 33 [Ps. 33]: “I will bless the Lord,” and the Dismissal.

§. 66.

Concerning the All-Night Vigil.

The All-Night Vigil consisteth of Great Vespers with Litiya and of Matins with the 1st Hour,—or of Great Compline with Litiya and of Matins with the 1st Hour. We do not intend to describe the order of this divine service here yet again, for each one will already know it from the things spoken. Here let us only note this: that if the All-Night Vigil beginneth in such wise that the Priest, having opened the royal doors and censed the church, at the royal doors at “Command” [*Povelite*] proclaimeth: “Glory to the Holy,” and thus “Come, let us worship” (thrice); then at the beginning of Matins let him not proclaim anew “Glory to the Holy,” but instead of this let him say: “Blessed and glorified is Christ our God,” and thus let there follow: “Glory to God in the highest” (twice). If, however, at the beginning of Matins there is to be said: “Glory to the Holy,” then at the end of the Litiya let there be said: “The blessing of the Lord be upon you.”

§. 67.

What is Needful to Note Concerning the Church Rule [*Pravilo*].

In the Western Latin Church there is a law (*lex* [law]), according to which not only a Priest, but also a subdeacon daily (*quotidie* [daily]) under mortal sin (*sub peccato mortali* [under mortal sin]) is bound to celebrate the Church Rule (*Breviarium* [Breviary]). Moreover, those ecclesiastical persons who also hold some ecclesiastical benefice not only sin if they do not complete the Church Rule, but also are bound in conscience to give the corresponding portion of their revenues unto pious purposes, that is, they are bound to celebrate the Church Rule not only under mortal sin, but also under obligation of restitution (*sub onere restitutionis* [under obligation of restitution]).

In the Eastern Greek Church, however, there is no such clear definition. There are those who, according to the words of the Constitution of Benedict XIV *“Eo quamvis,”* say this: that among us the obligation to fulfill the Church Rule hath arisen not by express law (*nulla lege expressa* [by no express law]), but by praiseworthy custom (*laudabili consuetudine* [by praiseworthy custom]). Yet among us it is not even known whether a presbyter, or already also a subdeacon, is bound unto this. Further, neither is it known when a priest is bound unto this: whether daily (*quotidie* [daily]), or only when he celebrateth the Liturgy? It seemeth to me that it is a praiseworthy matter to celebrate the Rule daily (*quotidie* [daily]); but it appeareth that it is prescribed under mortal sin (*sub peccato mortali* [under mortal sin]) only when the Priest celebrateth the Holy Liturgy. For so it is found in the Oriental Code, law 66a (*in Codice Orientali lege 66-a*): *“Sacerdos non recitans horas canonicas et celebrat, peccat; si suppleverit, remittitur peccatum”* [“A priest who doth not recite the canonical hours and celebrateth, sinneth; if he shall have supplied them, the sin is remitted”] (Papp-Szilágyi: *Enchiridion juris Eccl. Orient.*, Magno-Varadini, 1862, p. 77). Likewise it is not known whether a priest who hath a church parish is obliged, if he doth not complete the Church Rule, to give the due portion from his revenues unto pious purposes, that is, whether he is bound to fulfill the rule under obligation of restitution (*sub onere restitutionis* [under obligation of restitution]), or not? Finally, since among us the Church Rule is lengthy and requireth many books for its conscientious completion, and in many places books are not even to be had: therefore it is not known how many of the services a priest ought to complete. A full rule, or an abbreviated one? Unto this question Pope Pius IX answered two priests thus: *“Sequantur praxim aliorum piorum Sacerdotum illius regionis”* [“Let them follow the practice of other pious priests of that region”] (cf. *Zeitschrift für Kath. Theolog.*, VII. Jahrgang, I. Heft, 1883, p. 188).

## CHAPTER II.

§. 68.

Concerning the Church Rule According to Diverse Occurrences.

In this chapter, in three articles concerning the daily services, that is, concerning the daily rule, we shall speak in such wise that: 1) Sunday shall be the head, and unto it we shall adapt the diverse occurrences. 2) The weekday shall be the head, and unto it we shall adapt the diverse occurrences. Finally: 3) Saturday shall be our subject according to diverse occurrences.

### ARTICLE I.

§. 69.

I.) If It Be Sunday and a Small Saint (at 3).

(α) On Saturday at Great Vespers: The usual beginning, the litany of peace. Kathisma: “Blessed is the man,” entire. Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” at 10, and that: 7 of the Resurrection from the Octoechos, and to the Saint 3 from the Anthologion or from the Common Menaion [*Obshchina*]. “Glory”: to the Saint, if he have one; if, however, he have none: “Glory,” and “Now and”: The Dogmatikon of the current tone from the Octoechos. Great entrance. “O Joyful Light.” Great Prokimenon (“The Lord reigneth”). During the Prokimenon the Priest cometh unto the Holy Table, and after its singing singeth the litany: “Let us all say.” After this the chanter readeth: “Vouchsafe, O Lord,” upon the conclusion of which the Priest singeth the second litany: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha 4, and those of the Resurrection from the Octoechos. (Unto the Saint we take nothing.) “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection from the aposticha according to the tone of the Saint. If, however, there is no sticheron written for the Saint at “Glory,” then “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion from the aposticha. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection from the troparia of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Great dismissal.

(β) At Small Compline: All things as usual. Canon to the Theotokos. Hypakoe of the tone of the day. Unto the Saint nothing is taken; he is commemorated only in the Dismissal.

(γ) At the Midnight Office: All things as usual. Triadikon Canon. The Saint is commemorated only in the Dismissal.

(δ) At Matins: The usual beginning. At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection (twice). “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Kathisma 1. Sessional Hymn 1. Kathisma 2. Sessional Hymn 2. Kathisma 3.[^153] (All Sessional Hymns are taken from the Octoechos.) Instead of the 3rd Sessional Hymn: The troparia “The angelic assembly was astonished.” — After this the small litany. Hypakoe of the tone. The Graduals [*Stepenny*]. During this the Priest cometh vested in the phelonion unto the Holy Table, and there proclaimeth: “Let us attend, peace be to all, wisdom, let us attend,” and the chanter singeth the Prokimenon. At “Let us pray to the Lord” the Priest openeth the royal doors—and the rest as usual. The Gospel. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ.” Psalm 50 [Ps. 50] with the stichera. The Canon is fourfold and is taken at 14 (of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Cross and Resurrection at 3, of the Theotokos at 3, and to the Saint at 4). Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the Canon and the litany: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. At Ode 8: “My soul doth magnify.” “More honorable than the Cherubim,” etc. After Ode 9: The small litany, after which the Priest proclaimeth:[^154] “Holy is the Lord our God”; the chanter the same (twice), and the Exapostilarion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Exapostilarion of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. “Let every breath” and the stichera on the Praises 8 from the Octoechos. (Unto the Saint nothing is taken.) “Glory”: Gospel sticheron of the day. “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. The Trisagion. Troparion of the Resurrection: “Today salvation is come unto the world,” or “Having risen from the tomb.” The litanies: “Have mercy on us, O God,” and “Let us complete.” Great dismissal.

(ε) At the Hours and at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Resurrection. — At the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint. “Now and”: “O Theotokos, thou art [the vine].” After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Saint. — At the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the temple; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the temple. At the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; “Now and”: “He Who for our sake,” etc.; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection, etc., as usual. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: Kontakion of the Sunday, of the Temple, and to the Saint. In the dismissals the Saint is commemorated everywhere.

§. 70.

II.) If It Be Sunday and a Middle Saint (at 6).

(α) At Vespers: Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” at 10, and that: 3 of the Resurrection, 1 of the East, and to the Saint 6.[^155] “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion. Stichera on the aposticha all of the Resurrection. If the Saint have a “Glory,” then “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone of the Saint. If, however, the Saint have no “Glory,” then “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. Great dismissal;

(β) At Small Compline: Canon to the Theotokos according to the tone. After Ode 9 of the Canon: “It is meet and right.” After this: The Trisagion. “Our Father” with the exclamation. Hypakoe of the tone. The Saint is commemorated only in the Dismissal;

(γ) At the Midnight Office: Triadikon Canon. The Saint is commemorated only in the Dismissal;

(δ) At Matins: The usual beginning. At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection (twice). “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Kathismata and Sessional Hymns of the Resurrection as usual. Hypakoe. Graduals [*Stepenny*]. Prokimenon. Gospel of the Resurrection, the rest in order. The Canon at 14, and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Cross and Resurrection at 2, of the Theotokos at 2, and to the Saint at 6. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the Canon: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Sunday. After Ode 9: The small litany. “Holy is the Lord our God” and the Exapostilarion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion from the Sunday Exapostilarion. “Let every breath,” etc., and the stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection (from the Octoechos), and to the Saint 4 with the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*], that is, the sticheron of the Saint appointed at “Glory” is taken as the fourth sticheron. After this “Glory”: Gospel sticheron. “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. Troparion of the Resurrection, only one of the two. Litanies 2. Great dismissal;

(ε) At the Hours and at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: All things are celebrated as on a Sunday if there be a small Saint. In the dismissals the Saint is commemorated everywhere.

§. 71.

III.) If It Be Sunday and a Great (Polyeleos) Saint.

(α) At Great Vespers: The usual beginning. Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” at 10, and that: 3 of the Resurrection, 1 of the East, and to the Saint 6. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion of the tone (that is, the 1st Theotokion). Great entrance with the censer. Great Prokimenon of the day. Readings 3 to the Saint, etc. Stichera on the aposticha all of the Resurrection from the Octoechos. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone of the same Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*]. “Now lettest Thou dismiss,” etc. Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone. All the rest in order;

(β) At Small Compline: Canon to the Theotokos and kontakion of the Saint alone. — At the Midnight Office: All things in order. The Saint is commemorated only in the Dismissals;

(γ) At Matins: The usual beginning. At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection (twice). “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Kathismata 2 with two Sunday Sessional Hymns and with their Theotokia. Instead of the 3rd Kathisma: The Polyeleos and the Magnification of the Saint with the selected verses. After the Magnification we do not sing “Glory,” and “Now and,” and “Alleluia,” but we sing immediately: the troparia after the Evlogitaria: “The angelic assembly.” The small litany. Sunday Hypakoe of the tone, and thus both Sessional Hymns to the Saint, once each, without Theotokia. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. Graduals [*Stepenny*]. Sunday Prokimenon. Gospel of the Resurrection. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ.” Psalm 50 [Ps. 50] and the Sunday sticheron. The Canon at 14, and that: of the Resurrection at 4 with the heirmos; of the Cross and Resurrection we do not take; of the Theotokos at 2, and to the Saint at 8. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the same. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. Before Ode 9: “My soul doth magnify” and “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: Small litany, and “Holy is the Lord our God,” and the Sunday Exapostilarion. After this “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection, and 4 to the Saint with the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*] and with its two refrains, which are written on the aposticha of Vespers. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron. “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. The Trisagion. Troparion of the Resurrection, only one of the two. All the rest as usual;

(δ) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection. At the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Saint; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the temple; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the temple; at the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection; — at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: Kontakion of the Resurrection, of the temple, and to the Saint. (In the dismissals the Saint is commemorated everywhere.)

§. 72.

IV.) If It Be Sunday and There Occur Two Small Saints Having an Office.

(α) At Great Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera at 10, and that: 4 of the Resurrection, to the 1st Saint 3, to the 2nd Saint 3.

“Glory”: to the 1st Saint; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion of the current tone (the 1st Theotokion). Great entrance. Prokimenon of the day: “The Lord reigneth”; on the aposticha the stichera of the Resurrection. “Glory”: to the 1st Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion from the aposticha of the Resurrection according to the tone of the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*] of the Saint. (If, however, the Saint have no Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*], then “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion.) After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection and the troparion of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Troparion of the [2nd] Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*] of the 2nd Saint. All the rest as usual;

(β) At the Midnight Office and at Small Compline: Unto the Saints nothing is taken. The Hypakoe and all things of the Sunday. The Saints are commemorated only in the dismissals;

(γ) At Matins: The usual beginning. At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection and the troparion of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Troparion of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the 2nd Saint. All the rest of the Resurrection from the Octoechos. The Canon at 14; and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Cross and Resurrection is omitted, of the Theotokos at 2, to the 1st Saint at 4, and to the 2nd Saint at 4. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3: Kontakion and ikos of the 1st and 2nd Saints, Sessional Hymn of the 1st Saint; “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. After Ode 9: Exapostilarion of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion from the Sunday Exapostilarion; on the Praises stichera at 8, and all of the Resurrection. (Unto the Saints nothing.) “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” After the Great Doxology and the Trisagion the troparion of the Resurrection: “Today salvation [is come unto the world],” or “Having risen from the tomb.” The rest in order;

(δ) At the Hours and at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: All things in order, that is: at the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Resurrection; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the 1st Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the 1st Saint; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the temple; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the temple; at the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the 2nd Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the 2nd Saint. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: Kontakion of the Resurrection and of the temple. “Glory”: Troparion of the 1st Saint; “Now and”: of the 2nd Saint. All the rest in order. In the dismissals the Saints are commemorated everywhere.

§. 73.

V.) If It Be Sunday and One Middle Saint (at 6) and One Small Saint (at 3).

(α) At Vespers: Here let us note that the small Saint is entirely omitted, and Vespers is celebrated only of Sunday and of the middle Saint, and that in such wise as was already set forth in the 2nd case;

(β) At Compline, however, the small Saint omitted at Vespers is taken, and that in such wise that with the Sunday canon the canon of the Saint is also combined. In each ode—that is—the heirmos of the Sunday canon is taken and its troparia are read with their refrain unto “Glory.” Before “Glory” the troparia from Ode 1 of the canon of the small Saint are read with its refrain, but without “Glory.” After this: “Glory,” and “Now and,” the troparion from the Sunday canon. At the conclusion of Ode 1 the heirmos of Ode 2 [Ode 3] of the Sunday canon is sung with its troparia and with the troparia of the small Saint. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Troparion from the Sunday canon. — And thus all the odes are taken. At the conclusion of the canon, instead of the stichera of the Resurrection, the stichera of the small Saint omitted at Vespers are sung;

(γ) At the Midnight Office: All things in order. The Saints are commemorated only in the dismissal;

(δ) At Matins: All things are celebrated of Sunday and to the middle Saint; the small Saint is commemorated only in the dismissal;

(ε) At the Hours all things are in order, just as when there are two Saints (as was set forth in the preceding cases). At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order.

§. 74.

VI.) If It Be Sunday and One Great Saint and One Small Saint.

(α) At Vespers and at Matins the small Saint is entirely omitted, and all things are celebrated of Sunday and to the great Saint;

(β) At Compline with the Sunday canon the canon of the small Saint is also celebrated. After “It is truly meet” [*Dostoyno*] the stichera of the small Saint omitted at Vespers are sung. All the rest as usual;

(γ) At the Midnight Office all things are as usual; the small Saint is commemorated only in the dismissal;

(δ) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the great Saint. After the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Resurrection; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the great Saint. “Glory”: Troparion of the small Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Hours. After the “Our Father,” kontakion of the great Saint; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the temple; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the temple; at the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the great Saint; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the small Saint. All the rest as usual.

§. 75.

VII.) If It Be Sunday and Many Small Saints.

In this case it is needful to note that those small Saints: either: (α) have their office together, and then all things will be celebrated just as for one Saint having stichera at 3 and a canon at 4; or: (β) each Saint hath his own proper office, and then only the office of the 1st Saint is taken, while the other Saints are omitted and commemorated only in the Dismissal.

§. 76.

VIII.) If It Be Sunday and a Great Saint Having a Litiya.

(α) At Little Vespers (on Saturday): Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” 4 from the Octoechos. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion of the tone of Little Vespers; on the aposticha one sticheron of the Resurrection from the Octoechos, and two of the Saint from Great Vespers with their refrains; “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone of Little Vespers. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. The smallest dismissal;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: The entire Kathisma (“Blessed is the man”). On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 4 from the Octoechos, and to the Saint 6; “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion of the current tone. Great entrance. Prokimenon of the day: “The Lord reigneth.” After the prokimenon, the Readings of the Saint. All the rest as usual. At the Litiya: Sticheron of the temple, and stichera of the Saint. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion in the same tone from the aposticha sung on Sunday. On the aposticha stichera of the Resurrection. “Glory”: to the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion in the same tone from the Sunday aposticha; — at the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion of the Saint twice, and the troparion “O Theotokos Virgin” once. All the rest as usual;

(γ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice; “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint. “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Kathismata. Sessional Hymns of the Resurrection with their refrains and Theotokia of the current tone. Polyeleos and Magnification of the Saint without “Glory,” and “Now and,” but immediately: The troparia: “The angelic assembly.” Hypakoe of the tone. Sessional Hymns 2 of the Saint. “Glory”: Polyeleos Sessional Hymn. “Now and”: Theotokion thereof. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of the tone. Prokimenon of the Sunday. Gospel of the Resurrection. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ.” Psalm 50 [Ps. 50] and so forth. “Glory”: “Through the prayers of the holy Apostles.” “Now and”: “Through the prayers of the Theotokos,” and the Sunday sticheron, and so forth. The Canon of the Resurrection at 4 with the heirmos, of the Theotokos at 4, and to the Saint at 8. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint. Sessional Hymn of the Saint. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection; at Ode 9: “My soul doth magnify” and “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Sunday Theotokion. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection, to the Saint 4 with the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*] and with two evening refrains written on the aposticha. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. The Trisagion. Only one of the two Sunday troparia. Litanies 2. Great dismissal.

(δ) At the Hours: All things are taken just as at another time when there is a Polyeleos Saint. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order. After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Kontakion of the Saint; “Now and”: Kontakion of the temple, if it be a temple of Christ or of the Theotokos; if, however, not, then: “O Protection of Christians.”

§. 77.

IX.) If It Be Sunday and a Feast of the Theotokos.[^156]

(α) At Little Vespers (on Saturday): On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 4. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. The stichera on the aposticha are taken thus: One sticheron of the Resurrection and 2 stichera of the feast from the aposticha of Great Vespers with the refrains of the feast. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast from Little Vespers. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. Litany: “Have mercy on us, O God.” The smallest dismissal;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: The usual beginning. Litany of Peace. The usual Kathismata. Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” 4 of the Resurrection, and to the feast 6. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. Great entrance. Prokimenon: “The Lord reigneth.” Readings 3 of the feast. Stichera on the Litiya 3 of the feast; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast; on the aposticha stichera of the Resurrection; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. After the Blessing of Loaves the troparion of the feast thrice. All the rest as usual;

(γ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Troparion of the feast. Kathisma 1. Sessional Hymn 1. Ka- thisma 2. Sessional Hymn 2; and all this of the Sunday. Instead of the 3rd Kathisma, the Polyeleos and the Magnification of the feast. After the Magnification we do not sing “Glory,” and “Now and,” and “Alleluia,” but immediately instead of the 3rd Sessional Hymn the troparia: “The angelic assembly.” Small litany. Sunday Hypakoe. After this follow the Sessional Hymns of the feast, and that: the 1st and the 2nd, all once each. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Polyeleos Sessional Hymn. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of the current Sunday tone. Prokimenon of the feast. Gospel of the feast. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ.” Psalm 50 [Ps. 50]. After this “Glory”: “Through the prayers of the Theotokos.” “Now and”: the same, and the sticheron of the feast. The Canon at 14, and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, that of the Cross and Resurrection is omitted, of the Theotokos at 2, and of the feast both canons at 8. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection and Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the feast. At Ode 9 the refrains of the feast are not sung, but “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: “Holy is the Lord our God.” Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Exaposteilarion of the feast. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection, 4 of the feast. “Glory”: of the feast; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. The Trisagion. The Sunday troparion only. Litanies 2. Great dismissal. (It is needful to note that here the Gospel of the Resurrection is omitted, and the Gospel sticheron, which is sung only after the blessing.)

(δ) There is no Midnight Office and Small Compline, for the Litiya is celebrated;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the feast; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Resurrection; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the feast; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the feast; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and troparion of the temple; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the temple; at the 9th Hour, just as at the 1st Hour; — at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order.

§. 78.

X.) If It Be Sunday and a Feast of the Lord.

It is proper to know that in this case nothing of the Sunday is sung, neither the stichera of the Resurrection, nor the Sunday troparia after the Evlogitaria [*po neporochnykh*], nor the Graduals [*Stepenny*] of the tone, nor the Gospel of the Resurrection, nor “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ,” nor “Holy is the Lord our God”; but the entire office is of the feast from the Menaion or from the Anthologion. The tone of the day, the Gospel of the Resurrection, the Gospel sticheron, and the Exaposteilarion are omitted, and on the following Sunday the following tone is taken; the feast, however, is taken:

(α) At Little Vespers: Instead of the Litany of Peace: “Lord, have mercy” (12 times); on “Lord, I have cried” stichera 4 of the feast. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. (There is no entrance.) Instead of the litany: “Let us complete,” there is said: “Lord, have mercy” (12 times). Stichera on the aposticha 3 of the feast. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the feast once. Litany: “Have mercy on us, O God.” Smallest festal dismissal;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: The usual beginning. Litany of Peace. The 1st Kathisma in its entirety;[^157] on “Lord, I have cried” all the stichera of the feast, and that at 8. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. Great entrance. Great Prokimenon: “The Lord reigneth.” Readings 3. Stichera on the Litiya 3 of the feast. Litany at the Litiya. Stichera on the aposticha all of the feast with their refrains. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the feast thrice without “Glory.” All the rest as usual. And immediately Matins beginneth;

(γ) There is no Midnight Office and Compline, for there is a Litiya;

(δ) At Matins: The beginning from “Glory to the Holy” (for it precedeth and is combined with the Litiya). The Six Psalms. Litany of Peace; at “God is the Lord” the troparion of the feast thrice with “Glory,” and “Now and.” Kathismata of the day (that is, the 2nd and 3rd). Sessional Hymns of the feast. Instead of the 3rd Kathisma, the Polyeleos. Magnification with verses. 3rd Sessional Hymn of the feast. Graduals [*Stepenny*] (1st antiphon of the 4th Tone: “From my youth”). Prokimenon and Gospel of the feast. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ” is omitted (except for the Exaltation and the Ascension). Psalm 50 [Ps. 50] with the stichera of the feast. One or two canons at 16, and that: the heirmos at 4, and the troparia at 12. Festal Katavasia, and that the heirmos of the 1st canon. After Ode 3: Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the feast; at Ode 9 instead of “The Theotokos and Mother,” etc., the Magnification of the feast is sung. After Ode 9: Exaposteilarion of the feast thrice. Stichera on the Praises either 4 or 6 of the feast. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. (There is no Gospel sticheron.) Great Doxology. The Trisagion. Troparion of the feast once. All the rest as usual;

(ε) At all the Hours the troparion of the feast. After the “Our Father,” the kontakion of the feast; — at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order, wherein after the “Our Father” the kontakion of the feast is taken.[^158]

§. 79.

XI.) If It Be Sunday and the Patronal Feast of a Male or Female Saint [Temple Feast].

In this case on Saturday evening and on Sunday at Matins all things are celebrated just as when on Sunday there occurs a Saint having a Litiya. Only here at Matins there are the prokimenon, Gospel, and sticheron of the temple.
On the evening of the same day there is the Apodosis for the honor of the temple, concerning which we note the following:
(α) At Vespers (that is, for Monday): On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the temple 3, to the Saint of the day falling on Monday 3. “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: Sunday Theotokion of the same tone. Prokimenon of the day: “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Litany: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha of the temple. “Glory”: of the temple or of the Saint; “Now and”: Sunday Theotokion. After the Trisagion, troparion of the temple. “Glory”: of the Saint (if there be one); “Now and”: Sunday Theotokion. Litany: “Have mercy on us, O God.” Small dismissal. And thus the service of the temple is concluded at the Apodosis without fail;

(β) At Matins there is no Apodosis as at Vespers. The entire office is to the Saint and of the day in order from the Octoechos.
Note: If on the morrow of a patronal Saint there occur a Sunday, a feast of Christ or of the Theotokos, or the feast of a great Saint having a Vigil or a Polyeleos, then in the evening there is no Apodosis of the temple.

§. 80.

XII.) If It Be Sunday, Forefeast, and One Saint.

(α) At Great Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 4, of the Forefeast 3, and to the Saint 3. “Glory”: to the Saint, if there be one; if, however, there be none, “Glory”: of the Forefeast; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion. Stichera on the aposticha of the Resurrection from the Octoechos. “Glory”: to the Saint, if there be one; if, however, there be none, “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the Forefeast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; “Now and”: of the Forefeast;

(β) At Small Compline (with the canon to the Theotokos): Hypakoe of the tone. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Kontakion of the Forefeast;

(γ) At the Midnight Office (with the Triadic Canon): All things in order;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice; “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; “Now and”: of the Forefeast. Kathismata of the day. Sessional Hymns of the Resurrection. (Those of the Forefeast are omitted.) Kathisma 17 (there is no Polyeleos), but immediately the troparia of the Resurrection: “The angelic assembly was amazed.” Sunday Graduals [*Stepenny*]. Prokimenon of the tone. Gospel of the Resurrection. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ.” Psalm 50 [Ps. 50], and the rest in order. The Canon at 14, and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4 (that of the Cross and Resurrection is omitted), of the Theotokos at 2, of the Forefeast at 4, and to the Saint at 4. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the Forefeast and to the Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Now and”: Sessional Hymn of the Forefeast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection; at Ode 9: “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: “Holy is the Lord our God” and Sunday Exaposteilarion. After this: “Glory”: Exaposteilarion of the Saint; “Now and”: Exaposteilarion of the Forefeast. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection, and 4 of the Forefeast with the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*]. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. After this the troparion of the Resurrection only, one of the two. All the rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the Forefeast; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Forefeast; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion of the Hours; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Resurrection; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the Forefeast; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Forefeast; at the 9th Hour, all things just as at the 3rd Hour. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are as usual.

§. 81.

XIII.) If It Be Sunday, Forefeast, and Two Saints.

In this case the 2nd Saint is transferred to Compline, where together with the canon to the Theotokos that Saint's canon and stichera are taken. The divine service is celebrated just as though there were only one Saint and the Forefeast. — (If, however, there be more Saints having proper offices, then of them only the service of the 1st Saint is celebrated, while all the other Saints are omitted and commemorated only in the dismissals.)

§. 82.

XIV.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast a Small Saint (at 3).

(α) At Great Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 4, of the feast 3, and to the Saint 3. “Glory”: to the Saint (if he have one), otherwise “Glory”: of the feast; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion. Stichera on the aposticha of the Resurrection 4. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. If, however, the Saint have no “Glory,” then “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. All the rest as usual;

(β) At Compline: Hypakoe of the tone. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Kontakion of the feast;

(γ) At the Midnight Office all things are in order;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Troparion of the feast. All the rest of the Resurrection. The Canon at 14; and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Theotokos at 2 (that of the Cross and Resurrection is omitted), of the feast at 4, and to the Saint at 4. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the feast and of the Saint; “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Now and”: Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. At “The Theotokos and Mother,” etc., “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: “Holy is the Lord our God,” and the Sunday Exaposteilarion; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection, 4 of the feast, of which two are with the usual refrains, and two with the refrains of the feast. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. The Trisagion. The troparion of the Resurrection only, one of the two. Great dismissal;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: Troparion of the feast; “Now and”: Theotokion of the Hours; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the feast; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: Troparion of the feast; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the feast; at the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: Kontakion of the Resurrection, of the temple, and of the feast.

§. 83.

XV.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast There Is a Middle Saint.

(α) At Great Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 3, of the feast 3, and to the Saint 4. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion. Prokimenon of the day. Stichera on the aposticha of the Resurrection 4. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. If, however, the Saint have no “Glory,” then: “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast;

(β) At Compline: Hypakoe of the tone. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Kontakion of the feast;

(γ) At the Midnight Office: All things are celebrated in order (that is, of the Sunday);

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Troparion of the feast. All the rest of the Resurrection. The Canon at 14, and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the feast at 4, and to the Saint at 6. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the feast and of the Saint; “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Now and”: Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection; at “The Theotokos and Mother,” etc., “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: “Holy is the Lord our God” and immediately the Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. On the Praises stichera of the Resurrection 4, and of the feast 4. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” All the rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours just as was said above when there is a small Saint;

(ϛ) At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are as usual.

§. 84.

XVI.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast a Great (Polyeleos) Saint.

(α) At Great Vespers: Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” at 10, and that: of the Resurrection 3, of the feast 3, and to the Saint 4. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast; on the aposticha stichera of the Resurrection 4. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss” the troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast;

(β) At Compline: Hypakoe of the Sunday. Kontakion of the Saint. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Kontakion of the feast. All the rest in order;

(γ) At the Midnight Office all things are in order;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. Kathismata of the day. Sessional Hymns of the Resurrection with their Theotokia. Polyeleos. Magnification. And (not singing “Glory,” and “Now and”) immediately the troparia: “The angelic assembly.” Hypakoe of the tone. Sessional Hymns of the Saint. After the 1st and 2nd stichology, once each without Theotokia, immediately “Glory”: Polyeleos Sessional Hymn; “Now and”: Ses- sional Hymn of the feast. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of the tone. Sunday Prokimenon of the tone. Gospel of the Resurrection of the day. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ,” and the rest in order. The Canon of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the feast at 4, to the Saint at 6. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the feast, and kontakion and ikos of the Saint; “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Now and”: Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. At Ode 9: “My soul doth magnify” and “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: “Holy is the Lord,” etc., Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the Resurrection, to the Saint 4 with two refrains from the evening aposticha. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. The Trisagion. The Sunday troparion only, one of the two;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: Troparion of the feast; “Now and”: Theotokion of the Hours; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the feast; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: to the Saint; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Saint; at the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: of the feast; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the feast; at the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: to the Saint; after the “Our Father,” kontakion of the Saint. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are as usual.

§. 85.

XVII.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast There Is a Saint Having a Litiya.

(α) At Little Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 4 from the Octoechos; “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast; on the aposticha one sticheron of the Resurrection and 2 of the Saint from the aposticha of Great Vespers with their refrains. “Glory”: to the Saint from the aposticha of Little Vespers; “Now and”: of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Resurrection. “Glory”: Troparion of the Saint; “Now and”: Troparion of the feast;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 3, of the feast 3, and to the Saint 4. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion of the current tone. Readings 3. At the Litiya: Sticheron of the feast and stichera of the Saint; “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast; on the aposticha stichera of the Resurrection from the Octoechos. “Glory”: to the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. At the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion of the Saint twice, and the troparion “O Theotokos Virgin” once (without “Glory”);

(γ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Troparion of the feast. After the kathismata, Sessional Hymns of the Resurrection with their Theotokia. All the rest both at Matins and at the Hours is in order, just as for a great (Polyeleos) Saint.

§. 86.

XVIII.) If It Be Sunday, and Within a Feast There Are Two Small Saints.

(α) At Vespers the office of the 2nd Saint is omitted and is placed at Compline. Vespers concludes in the same manner as when it is Sunday and within a feast there is one small Saint;

(β) At Compline with the Canon of the Theotokos the Canon of the Saint is also combined. With the Sunday stichera the stichera of the Saint are also taken. Hypakoe of the tone; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast;

(γ) At the Midnight Office all things are in order;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice and the troparion of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Troparion of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. All the rest of the Resurrection. The Canon at 14, and that: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Theotokos at 2, of the feast at 4, and to the 1st Saint at 4. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the feast and of the 1st Saint. After this, Sessional Hymn of the 1st Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. After Ode 9: Sunday Exaposteilarion; “Glory”: Exaposteilarion of the 1st Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. On the Praises stichera of the Resurrection 4, of the feast 4 with their verses. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. And the rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the feast. After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the feast. At the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the 1st Saint. After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection and of the 1st Saint. At the 6th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the feast; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the feast. At the 9th Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection and of the 2nd Saint; at the end: Kontakion of the Resurrection and of the 2nd Saint.

§. 87.

XIX.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast There Are Two Saints; Namely: One Middle and One Small.

(α) At Vespers: The office of the small Saint is omitted and is placed at Compline. Vespers is celebrated in the same manner as when it is Sunday and within a feast there is a middle Saint;

(β) At Compline: With the Canon of the Theotokos the Canon of the small Saint is combined. After “It is truly meet” the stichera of the small Saint are sung. Further: Hypakoe of the Resurrection. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Kontakion of the feast; all the rest is in order;

(γ) At the Midnight Office all things are in order;

(δ) At Matins[^159] at “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice. “Glory”: Troparion of the middle Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. All the rest of the Resurrection. The Canon of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Theotokos at 2, of the feast at 4, and of the 1st Saint at 4. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the feast and of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the 1st Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. After Ode 9: Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory”: Exaposteilarion of the 1st Saint; “Now and”: Exaposteilarion of the feast. On the Praises stichera of the Resurrection 4, and of the feast 4 (of the Saint nothing). “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” The rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours and at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are as when there is a middle Saint within a feast.

§. 88.

XX.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast There Are Two Saints; Namely: One Great and One Small.[^160]

(α) At Vespers: The small Saint is omitted and transferred to Compline; and Vespers is celebrated in the same manner as when it is Sunday and within a feast there is one great Saint;

(β) At Compline: The Canon of the Resurrection is combined with the Canon of the small Saint. After “It is truly meet,” stichera of the small Saint. After this: Trisagion. “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Hypakoe of the Resurrection. Kontakion of the feast and of the great Saint;

(γ) At the Midnight Office: All things as usual (that is, of the Sunday);

(δ) At Matins at “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice. “Glory”: Troparion of the great Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. All the rest of the Resurrection from the Octoechos. The Canon at 14; namely: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4 (the Cross-Resurrection canon is omitted, the Canon of the Theotokos likewise), of the feast at 4, and of the Saint at 6. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the feast and of the Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Now and”:

of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection; at “The Theotokos and Mother,” etc., “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: “Holy is the Lord our God” and Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the feast. On the Praises stichera of the Resurrection 4, and of the Saint 4. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. Only the troparion of the Resurrection, one of the two. All the rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours: All things as was said above.

§. 89.

XXI.) If It Be Sunday and Within a Feast There Is a Temple of a Saint (Male or Female).

In this case the office of the Sunday and of the Temple is celebrated, and that in the same manner as for a great Saint. (The afterfeast is omitted.)

(α) Vespers with Litiya of the Sunday and of the Temple takes place, at which nothing of the afterfeast is taken;

(β) At Matins: Polyeleos, Sessional Hymn of the temple, and to it only “Now and”: of the feast. Gospel of the temple, and the rest. Katavasia of the day. Gospel sticheron;

(γ) At the Hours: Of the Sunday and of the Temple.

§. 90.

XXII.) If It Be Sunday, the Apodosis of a Feast, and One Saint.

In this case the office of the Saint is omitted entirely, and is concluded one day prior (except the “Entry of the Most Holy Virgin”). And only the office of the Sunday and of the Feast is taken; and that in this manner:

(α) At Great Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 4, of the feast 6, sung on the feast itself. “Glory”: of the feast; “Now and”: Dogmatikon of the tone. Stichera on the aposticha all of the Resurrection 4. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Resurrection once. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast;

(β) At Compline: Hypakoe of the tone. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast;

(γ) At the Sunday Midnight Office all things are in order;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. All the rest of the Resurrection. The Canon at 14; namely: of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the Theotokos at 2, of the feast at 8. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3: Kon- takion and ikos of the feast; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Sessional Hymn of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Resurrection. At Ode 9: “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: Sunday Exaposteilarion. “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. On the Praises stichera of the Resurrection 4, of the feast 4 with the Doxastikon [*Slavny*] and with the refrains of the feast. “Glory”: Gospel sticheron; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” After the Great Doxology the troparion of the Resurrection only, one of the two. The rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours and at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are as usual of the Sunday and of the feast. (That is: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: Troparion of the feast. After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the Resurrection and of the feast alternating.)

§. 91.

XXIII.) If It Be Sunday, the Apodosis of a Feast, and Two Saints.

In this case the office of one Saint is transferred to Compline, and of the other Saint is transferred to Compline of the preceding day; and then the entire office concludes just as though it were only the Sunday and the Apodosis of the feast. However, at Compline with the Sunday Canon the Canon of the 1st Saint is also combined, where his stichera are also taken. In the Dismissals the Saints are commemorated.

§. 92.

XXIV.) If It Be Sunday and with the Forefeast or with the Apodosis of Any Feast There Occur a Temple of a Saint (Male or Female).

(α) At Little Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection at 4; “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: Theotokion of Little Vespers; on the aposticha the sticheron of the Resurrection and the apostichon sticheron of the temple of the Saint with their refrains of Great Vespers. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us.” The smallest Dismissal;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: After the customary Psalm the entire Kathisma; on “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Resurrection 3, of the feast 3, of the temple 4. “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: Theotokion (the 1st of the tone). Entrance. Prokimenon of the day. Readings of the temple. — At the Litiya: Stichera of the temple. “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: of the feast. On the aposticha stichera of the Resurrection; “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: of the feast; at the Blessing of Loaves: Troparion of the temple twice, “O Theotokos Virgin” once. “Blessed be the name,” and the rest;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Resurrection twice. “Glory”: Troparion of the temple; “Now and”: of the feast. Kathismata. Sessional Hymns of the Resurrection with their Theotokia. Polyeleos and Sessional Hymns of the temple all once each. “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: of the feast. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of the tone. Prokimenon of the temple. Gospel of the temple. “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ,” and the rest. Sticheron of the temple, and the rest in order. Canon of the Resurrection with the heirmos at 4, of the feast at 4, of the temple at 6. Katavasia of the day. After Ode 3: Kontakion of the feast, Sessional Hymn of the temple; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the temple. At Ode 9: “More honorable than the Cherubim.” After Ode 9: Sunday Exaposteilarion; “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: of the feast; on the Praises stichera of the Resurrection 3, of the temple 2, and of the feast 3 with the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*]. The refrain we say of the feast. Then the Idiomelon sticheron of the feast we sing with its refrain. “Glory”: of the temple; “Now and”: “Most blessed art thou.” Great Doxology. Troparion of the Resurrection, one of the two. And the rest as usual;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: of the feast; “Now and”: Theotokion of the Hours. After the Trisagion: Kontakion of the Resurrection. At the 3rd Hour: Troparion of the Resurrection; “Glory”: of the temple. After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the temple. — Thus it takes place also at the other Hours, where first the troparion of the Resurrection is said, and then the troparion of the feast or of the temple; after the Trisagion: Kontakion of the temple, of the feast, and of the Resurrection, and that alternating. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order.

On the evening of the same day the Apodosis takes place for the honor of the temple, as was said above.

### ARTICLE II.

§. 93.

XXV.) If It Be a Weekday[^161] and a Small Saint (at 3).

(α) At Daily Vespers the beginning is usual. Litany of Peace. Fitting Kathisma. On “Lord, I have cried” stichera 3 from the Octoechos, and 3 to the Saint from the Menaion. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion written in the same place. If, however, the Saint hath a sticheron at “Glory,” then “Now and”: Theotokion from the Octoechos according to the tone and day. There is no entrance. Daily Prokimenon. “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha 3 from the Octoechos with the refrains of the Horologion [*Chasoslov*]. “Glory”: of the Saint (if he hath one); “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone (in the Octoechos on that same day). If, however, the Saint hath no “Glory,” then: “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion from the Octoechos.[^162] After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Saint once. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone and day. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us, O God.” Small daily Dismissal;

(β) At Compline the Canon of the Theotokos. After “It is truly meet”: Trisagion. “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Troparion of the temple (if it be a temple of Christ or of the Theotokos), troparion of the day and troparion: “O God of our Fathers.” “Glory”: Kontakion: “With the Saints”; “Now and”: “Through the intercessions, O Lord.” “Lord, have mercy” 40. Prayer: “Thou Who at all times.” (If, however, it be a temple of a Saint: then first the troparion of the day, after this of the temple of the Saint, then “O God of our Fathers,” and the rest;)[^163]

(γ) At the daily Midnight Office with the 17th Kathisma all things are in order;

(δ) At Matins: The beginning is usual. Troparion of the Saint twice. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Customary Kathismata. Sessional Hymns from the Octoechos. And immediately: “Lord, have mercy” 3. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Psalm 50. The Canon at 12; namely: from 3 canons: the 1st from the Octoechos with the heirmos at 4, the 2nd from the Octoechos at 4, and the 3rd of the Saint at 4. There is no Katavasia, but instead of it the heirmos from the canon of the Saint is taken, and that only after Ode 3, 6, 8, and 9. After Ode 3 of the canon: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion (on Wednesday and Friday: Stavrotheotokion). After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint. After Ode 9: Exaposteilarion from the Octoechos. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion thereof (on Wednesday and Friday: Exaposteilarion of the day; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Stavrotheotokion). On the Praises there are no stichera, but the Psalms are read. Small Doxology. Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha from the Octoechos 3 of the day (and that, two with the refrains of the Horologion [*Chasoslov*]). “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion from the Octoechos according to the tone of the Saint. “It is good to give praise,” etc. Trisagion. “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Troparion of the Saint. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone and day. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us, O God.” Daily Dismissal;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion of the day; after the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the day. At the 3rd Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the Saint. At the 6th Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the temple. At the 9th Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the Saint. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order.

§. 94.

XXVI.) If It Be a Weekday and a Middle Saint (at 6).

(α) At Daily Vespers: Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” 6 of the Saint. (From the Octoechos nothing is taken.) “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*]. There is no entrance. Prokimenon of the day. “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha 3 from the Octoechos, with the refrains from the Horologion [*Chasoslov*]. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion. At the end, troparion of the Saint. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone and day. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us, O God.” Small Dismissal;

(β) At Compline, and (γ) At the Midnight Office all things are in order, as was said above when a small, that is, non-festal Saint occurreth;

(δ) At Matins the beginning is usual. At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Saint twice. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion on that day according to the tone of the troparion of the Saint. Kathismata of the day and Sessional Hymns from the Octoechos. Psalm 50. The Canon at 14; namely: the 1st from the Octoechos with the heirmos at 4, the 2nd from the Octoechos at 4, and the 3rd of the Saint at 6. Instead of a Katavasia the heirmos from the canon of the Saint, and that only after Ode 3, 6, 8, and 9. After Ode 3: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint. After Ode 9: Exaposteilarion from the Octoechos. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion or Stavrotheotokion. On the Praises stichera of the Saint at 4 (if he hath any).[^164] “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion. Small Doxology. Stichera on the aposticha from the Octoechos; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion on that day in the Octoechos. After “It is good to give praise” troparion of the Saint once. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion at the end of Matins. The rest as usual. But if the six-stichera Saint hath a Great Doxology, then after the Doxology followeth the Trisagion. Troparion of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. Ektenias: “Let us complete” and “Have mercy on us, O God,” etc.;

(ε) At the Hours and at the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order, as was said above.

§. 95.

XXVII.) If It Be a Weekday and a Polyeleos Saint (at 8).

(α) Vespers is celebrated from the Anthologion or from the Menaion (the Octoechos is omitted), and it is Great with an entrance and with a Great Dismissal. It is celebrated thus: Customary beginning. Introductory Psalm [Ps. 103]. Litany of Peace; on “Lord, I have cried” stichera 8 from the Anthologion of the Saint. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Dogmatikon Theotokion according to the tone of the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*] of the Saint. (On Friday evening, however: “Now and”: Dogmatikon of the preceding Sunday.) Great Entrance. Prokimenon of the day. Readings 3. Ektenia: “Let us all say.” “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha from the Anthologion of the Saint with his verses. “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone from the Sunday aposticha. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Trisagion. “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Troparion of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. Great Dismissal;

(β) At Compline: Kontakion of the Saint only;

(γ) At the daily Midnight Office the 17th Kathisma with the customary troparia is taken in order;

(δ) At Matins: It is Great, with a Polyeleos, with the Gospel, and with a Great Dismissal. It is celebrated thus: Beginning as usual; at “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Saint twice; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. Kathismata of the day and Sessional Hymns of the Saint from the Menaion. (1st Kathisma, 1st Sessional Hymn; 2nd Kathisma, 2nd Sessional Hymn.) Instead of the 3rd Kathisma, Polyeleos and Magnification of the Saint. 3rd Sessional Hymn of the Saint. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of Tone 4 (“From my youth”). Prokimenon of the Saint. Gospel of the Saint. Ektenia: “Save, O God.” Canon at 14; namely: one of the Theotokos at 6, and of the celebrated Saint at 8. Katavasia after each ode of the day. After Ode 3 of the canon: Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the Saint; at Ode 9 there is sometimes a Magnification of the Saint. After Ode 9: Exaposteilarion of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: in the same place (“Holy is the Lord our God” is not sung). Stichera on the Praises of the Saint at 4 with “Glory,” and “Now and” from the Anthologion or from the Menaion. Great Doxology. Troparion of the Saint once. “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion of the Resurrection. Two ektenias. Great Dismissal;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st, 3rd, and 9th Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the Saint; at the 6th Hour, however: Troparion and kontakion of the temple. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order.

§. 96.

XXVIII.) If It Be a Weekday and Two Small Saints.

(α) At Daily Vespers: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the 1st Saint at 3, and of the 2nd Saint 3. “Glory”: of the 1st Saint, if he hath one; but if not, “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion which followeth the stichera of the 2nd Saint. There is no entrance. “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha from the Octoechos. “Glory”: of the 1st Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion in the same tone. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Troparion of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone of the 2nd Saint. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us, O God.” Daily Dismissal;[^165]

(β) At Compline all things are in order, but only this is necessary to note, that the troparion of the temple is taken if the temple be of Christ or of the Theotokos, and the troparion of the day. If, however, it be a temple of a Saint, then first the troparion of the day is taken, and then the troparion of the temple, and then “O God of our Fathers”;

(γ) At the Midnight Office all things are in order. The Saints are commemorated only in the Dismissal;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the 1st Saint twice. “Glory”: Troparion of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone and day. The rest as usual. (1st Kathisma, 1st Sessional Hymn; 2nd Kathisma, 2nd Sessional Hymn from the Octoechos.) Psalm 50. The Canon at 14; namely: the 1st from the Octoechos with the heirmos at 6 (the second canon from the Octoechos is omitted), of the 1st Saint at 4, of the 2nd Saint at 4. Katavasia after Ode 3, 6, 8, and 9 is the heirmos of the canon of the 2nd Saint. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion and ikos of the 2nd Saint and Sessional Hymn of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Sessional Hymn of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. After Ode 6: Kontakion and ikos of the 1st Saint. After Ode 9: Exaposteilarion of the day from the Octoechos and of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”:

Theotokion of the day. The “Praise the Lord” Psalms [Pss. 148–150], etc., are only read. Small Doxology. Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera on the aposticha from the Octoechos. “Glory”: of the Saint, if there be one; “Now and”: Theotokion according to the tone of the Saint. If the Saint hath no Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*], then “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion from the Octoechos. Psalm: “It is good to give praise” [Ps. 91:2]. Trisagion. “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Troparion of the 1st Saint. “Glory”: Troparion of the 2nd Saint; “Now and”: Dismissal Theotokion according to the tone and day. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us.” Small Dismissal;

(ε) At the Hours: At the 1st Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the day; at the 3rd Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the 1st Saint; at the 6th Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the temple; at the 9th Hour: Troparion and kontakion of the 2nd Saint. — At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*] all things are in order.

§. 97.

XXIX.) If It Be a Weekday and Two Saints; Namely: One Middle or Great Saint and One Small.

In these cases the small Saint is transferred to Compline, where his stichera and his canon are taken. The rest of Divine Worship is celebrated just as though it were only a weekday and a middle Saint (at 6) or a great Saint (at 8).

§. 98.

XXX.) If It Be a Weekday and Many Saints.

The Saints either: (α) Together have one office, and then the entire service concludes just as though there were only one Saint; or: (β) Each one of them hath his own particular office, and then only the 1st Saint is taken, while all the others are omitted and are commemorated only in the Dismissals.

§. 99.

XXXI.) If It Be a Weekday and a Saint Having a Litiya.

In this case the Octoechos is omitted and the entire office is taken from the Menaion or from the Anthologion.

(α) At Small Vespers: Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” at 4; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion. On the aposticha, stichera of the Saint; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Resurrection Theotokion;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the Saint at 8; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Dogmatikon according to the tone of the Doxastikon [*Naslavnyk*]. (If, however, it is Saturday: then “Now and”: Theotokion-Dogmatikon of the incoming tone.) Prokimenon of the day. Three Readings of the Saint. — At the Litiya: Sticheron of the temple and stichera of the Saint; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Resurrection Theotokion according to the tone of the Saint; on the aposticha, stichera and “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: Theotokion in the same place, or from the Sunday aposticha of the same tone; at the blessing of the loaves: Troparion of the Saint twice, and “O Theotokos Virgin” once. “Blessed be the name of the Lord” 3 times. Psalm 33 [Ps. 33]: “I will bless the Lord at all times,” etc., unto “...do not lack any good thing.” The rest as usual;

(γ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the Saint twice; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Resurrection Theotokion according to the tone. After the kathismata and after the sessional hymns, the Polyeleos, and then the 3rd Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: Theotokion. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of Tone 4, the first antiphon (“From my youth”). Prokimenon, Gospel, and Sticheron of the Saint. The Canon from the Octoechos of the Theotokos with the heirmos at 6, and of the Saint without the heirmos at 8. (If it is Saturday: then the Canon of the temple of Christ or of the Theotokos at 6, and of the celebrated Saint at 8; the Canon of the Theotokos in the Octoechos is omitted.) Katavasia of the day after each ode. At Ode 9 is sung: “More honorable than the Cherubim.” All the rest in order, as at Matins of a Polyeleos Saint;

(δ) At the Hours all things are in order. At the Typika [*Obidnytsia*]: Kontakion of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the temple of Christ or of the Theotokos. (If, however, it is a temple of a Saint, then the kontakion of the temple; “Glory”: Kontakion of the Saint; “Now and”: “Steadfast Protectress of Christians,” etc.)

§. 100.

XXXII.) If a Feast of the Theotokos Fall on a Weekday.

Since feasts of the Theotokos for the most part all have a Litiya, therefore at these feasts Compline and the Midnight Office are omitted. The festal Divine Worship is celebrated thus:

(α) At Small Vespers: The beginning is as usual. Instead of the Peace Ektenia, 12 times “Lord, have mercy.” There is no Kathisma. On “Lord, I have cried” stichera of the feast 4; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. There is no entrance. Prokimenon of the day. “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Instead of the ektenia: “Let us complete,” 12 times “Lord, have mercy”; on the aposticha, stichera of the feast from the Anthologion or from the Menaion; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast; after “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Trisagion, “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Troparion of the feast once. Ektenia: “Have mercy on us.” The smallest Dismissal;

(β) At Great Vespers with Litiya: The beginning is as usual. Peace Ektenia. (If it is Monday, from the Kathisma: “Blessed is the man” [Ps. 1], the 1st Antiphon; on the other days, however, the kathisma is not sung, only on Sunday the entire kathisma.) Stichera on “Lord, I have cried” of the feast 8; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast from the Anthologion. Great Entrance. Prokimenon of the day. Three Readings. Ektenia: “Let us say with our whole soul.” “Vouchsafe, O Lord.” Ektenia: “Let us complete.” Stichera at the Litiya of the feast; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. Ektenia at the Litiya. Stichera on the aposticha of the feast with the verses of the feast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Trisagion and “Our Father” with the ecphonesis. Troparion of the feast 3 times (without “Glory”). Prayer at the blessing of the loaves. “Blessed be the name of the Lord.” And immediately begins:

(γ) Matins from “Glory to the holy.” After this: Six Psalms. Peace Ektenia. At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the feast of the Theotokos with “Glory,” and “Now and” 3 times. After the usual kathismata, Sessional Hymns of the feast. Instead of the 3rd Kathisma, the Polyeleos and the Magnification with verses, then the refrain. 3rd Sessional Hymn. Graduals [*Stepenny*] of Tone 4, the 1st Antiphon (“From my youth”). Prokimenon and Gospel of the feast. Psalm 50 [Ps. 50] with the sticheron of the feast. Ektenia: “Save, O God.” The Canon (or 2 canons) at 14, which is celebrated in order, as it is in the Anthologion or Menaion. Katavasia of the day; after each ode the heirmos of the 1st canon. At Ode 9 the refrain of the feast is sung with the heirmos of Ode 9. Exaposteilarion of the feast 3 times. Stichera on the Praises 4 of the feast; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the feast. Great Doxology. After this, Trisagion. Troparion of the feast once; the rest as usual;

(δ) At all the Hours: Troparion of the feast. After the “Our Father”: Kontakion of the feast.

§. 101.

XXXIII.) If a Feast of the Lord Fall on a Weekday.

In this case all things are celebrated in such manner as is set forth in Case XXXII, only this is necessary to note, that there are certain Feasts of the Lord in which at Matins after the Gospel “Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ” is also taken (the Exaltation of the Precious Cross and the Ascension).[^166]

§. 102.

XXXIV.) If the Feast of the Temple of a Saint (Male or Female) Fall on Weekdays.

In this case (except on Sunday) all things are celebrated in such manner as when there is a Saint having a Litiya.

§. 103.

XXXV.) If Forefeast and One Saint Fall on Weekdays.

(α) At Vespers the Octoechos is omitted, and stichera on “Lord, I have cried” are sung: of the forefeast 3, and of the Saint 3; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the forefeast. (On Friday evening, the Dogmatikon of the preceding Sunday, except for the Nativity and Theophany.) On the aposticha, stichera of the forefeast; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the forefeast. After “Now lettest Thou dismiss”: Troparion of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the forefeast;

(β) At Compline: Kontakion of the forefeast;

(γ) At the Midnight Office: All things in order with the usual troparia;

(δ) At Matins: At “God is the Lord” the troparion of the forefeast twice; “Glory”: of the Saint; “Now and”: of the forefeast. After the kathismata, Sessional Hymns of the forefeast. The Canon of the forefeast with the heirmos at 8, and of the Saint at 4. After Ode 3 of the canon: Kontakion of the Saint and Sessional Hymn of the Saint; “Glory,” and “Now and”: of the forefeast.
